arazzos:
- description: Create a Jira issue, assign it to a user, then read it back to confirm the assignee.
  name: Atlassian Jira Create and Assign Issue
  slug: atlassian-create-and-assign-issue-workflow
- description: Create a Confluence page, read its version, then publish an updated body.
  name: Atlassian Confluence Create and Update Page
  slug: atlassian-create-and-update-page-workflow
- description: Confirm a parent page, create a child page under it, then read the child back.
  name: Atlassian Confluence Create Child Page
  slug: atlassian-create-child-page-workflow
- description: Create a Jira issue, read it back, then add an opening comment.
  name: Atlassian Jira Create Issue and Comment
  slug: atlassian-create-issue-and-comment-workflow
- description: Create a Jira issue, list its available transitions, then move it to a new status.
  name: Atlassian Jira Create Issue and Transition
  slug: atlassian-create-issue-and-transition-workflow
- description: Create a Confluence page, tag it with a label, then read it back.
  name: Atlassian Confluence Create Page with Label
  slug: atlassian-create-page-with-label-workflow
- description: Create a Confluence page in a space and read it back to confirm it.
  name: Atlassian Confluence Create Page
  slug: atlassian-create-page-workflow
- description: Stand up a new Jira project and seed it with its first issue.
  name: Atlassian Jira Create Project and First Issue
  slug: atlassian-create-project-and-first-issue-workflow
- description: Create a Jira project and read it back to confirm its details.
  name: Atlassian Jira Create Project
  slug: atlassian-create-project-workflow
- description: Create a Confluence space, confirm it, then add a first page to it.
  name: Atlassian Confluence Create Space and Page
  slug: atlassian-create-space-and-page-workflow
- description: Read an issue, edit its summary and description, then confirm the changes.
  name: Atlassian Jira Edit Issue Fields
  slug: atlassian-edit-issue-fields-workflow
- description: Create a Confluence space, confirm it, then list the content it contains.
  name: Atlassian Confluence Create Space and Inventory Content
  slug: atlassian-inventory-space-content-workflow
- description: Read a Confluence page and then list the comments attached to it.
  name: Atlassian Confluence Page and Comments
  slug: atlassian-page-comments-workflow
- description: Search Confluence content with CQL and fetch the first matching result.
  name: Atlassian Confluence Search Content with CQL
  slug: atlassian-search-content-cql-workflow
- description: Run a JQL search and fetch the full detail of the first matching issue.
  name: Atlassian Jira Search Issues with JQL
  slug: atlassian-search-issues-jql-workflow
- description: List an issue's transitions, move it to a new status, then record why with a comment.
  name: Atlassian Jira Transition Issue with Comment
  slug: atlassian-transition-issue-with-comment-workflow
- description: Read an existing issue, comment on it, then transition it to a triaged status.
  name: Atlassian Jira Triage Issue
  slug: atlassian-triage-issue-workflow

features:
- Jira (Software, Work Mgmt, Service Mgmt) per-user pricing
- Confluence per-user pricing
- Bitbucket Cloud per-user pricing
- Trello free + paid tiers
- Jira Product Discovery, Jira Align (separate)
- 'Most products: Free up to 10 users (5 for Bitbucket)'
- 'Standard tiers: Jira ~$9, Confluence ~$6, Bitbucket $3.65, JSM ~$23 per user/mo'
- Premium adds Atlassian Intelligence, advanced features, 99.9% SLA
- 'Enterprise: 99.95% uptime, data residency, centralized security'
- REST API at api.atlassian.com (per-product paths)
- GraphQL API (for some products)
- Per-app per-user rate limit ~10 req/sec
- OAuth 2.0 (3LO), API tokens, OAuth client credentials
- Atlassian Connect framework for marketplace apps
- Forge for serverless app dev
- Atlassian Marketplace ecosystem
finops:
- name: Atlassian Finops
  service_category: DevOps Platform
  slug: atlassian-finops
graphqls:
- description: ''
  name: Atlassian GraphQL API
  slug: atlassian-graphql
image: /assets/icons/atlassian.png
integrations:
- description: Real-time notifications and two-way integration between Atlassian products and Slack channels for team communication.
  name: Slack
- description: Integration with Microsoft Teams for Jira and Confluence notifications, issue creation, and collaboration.
  name: Microsoft Teams
- description: Connect GitHub repositories to Jira for linking commits, branches, and pull requests to Jira issues.
  name: GitHub
- description: Integration with GitLab for development information visibility within Jira issues.
  name: GitLab
- description: Official Atlassian VS Code extension for Jira issue management and Bitbucket code reviews from the IDE.
  name: VS Code
- description: Connect Jenkins CI/CD pipelines to Jira for build and deployment visibility on issues.
  name: Jenkins
- description: Terraform provider for managing Atlassian Operations resources as infrastructure-as-code.
  name: Terraform
- description: Incident management and on-call alerting integration now part of Jira Service Management Operations.
  name: Opsgenie
- description: Data Center Helm charts for deploying Atlassian products on Kubernetes clusters.
  name: Kubernetes
- description: Integration with Backstage developer portal for service catalog and component tracking.
  name: Backstage

overview: 'Atlassian publishes 320 APIs on the [APIs.io](https://apis.io/) network, including Account API, Activity API, Add API, and 317 more. Tagged areas include Code, Collaboration, Platform, Productivity, and Software Development.


  The Atlassian catalog on APIs.io includes 22 JSON-LD contexts and 2 Spectral governance rulesets.


  Atlassian''s developer surface includes authentication, developer portal, getting-started guide, changelog, support, engineering blog, YouTube channel, and 39 more developer resources.'
